Rashi on Exodus 12:39).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:3)


**יסבבנהו.** שָׁם סִבְּבָם וְהִקִּיפָם בַּעֲנָנִים וְסִבְּבָם בִּדְגָלִים לְאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת וְסִבְּבָן בְּתַחְתִּית הָהָר שֶׁכָּפָהוּ עֲלֵיהֶם כְּגִיגִית (עי' ספרי):


יסבבנהו HE SURROUNDED HIM — there He surrounded them and encompassed them with the “clouds of Glory”; He surrounded them with the banners on their four sides; he surrounded them by the underside of the mountain (Sinai), which He arched over them like a cask (cf. Sifrei Devarim 313:11).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:4)


**יבוננהו.** שָׁם בְּתוֹרָה וּבִינָה:


יבוננהו HE INSTRUCTED HIM there in Torah and wisdom.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:5)


**יצרנהו.** מִנָּחָשׁ שָׂרָף וְעַקְרָב וּמִן הָאֻמּוֹת:


יצרנהו HE GUARDED HIM from snakes, serpents and scorpions and from hostile nations,


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:6)


**כאישון עינו.** הוּא הַשָּׁחֹר שֶׁבָּעַיִן שֶׁהַמָּאוֹר יוֹצֵא הֵימֶנּוּ. וְאֻנְקְלוֹס תִּרְגֵּם יִמְצָאֵהוּ — "יַסְפִּיקֵהוּ כָּל צָרְכּוֹ בַמִּדְבָּר", כְּמוֹ (במדבר י"א) "מָצָא לָהֶם", (יהושע י"ז) "לֹא יִמָּצֵא לָנוּ הָהָר":


כאישון עינו AS THE אישון OF HIS EYE — that is the black in the eye from which the light comes. Onkelos, however, rendered ימצאהו by “He provided Him” with all He needed in the wilderness. It took the words to be of the same meaning as, (Numbers 11:22) “[Shall the flocks … be slain for them] to suffice (ומצא) them?” and (Joshua 17:16) “The hill is not enough (ימצא) for us”.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:7)


**יסבבנהו.** "אַשְׁרִינוּן סְחוֹר סְחוֹר לִשְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ", אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד בָּאֶמְצָע וְאַרְבָּעָה דְגָלִים לְאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת:


יסבבנהו — Onkelos renders this by “He made them abide round about His Divine dwelling place” — the “tent of congregation” in the middle and the divisions of the tribes (lit., the banners) on the four sides.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:1)


**כנשר יעיר קנו.** נִהֲגָם בְּרַחֲמִים וּבְחֶמְלָה, כַּנֶּשֶׁר הַזֶּה רַחְמָנִי עַל בָּנָיו, וְאֵינוֹ נִכְנָס לְקִנּוֹ פִּתְאֹם עַד שֶׁהוּא מְקַשְׁקֵשׁ וּמְטָרֵף עַל בָּנָיו בִּכְנָפָיו בֵּין אִילָן לְאִילָן, בֵּין שׂוֹכָה לַחֲבֶרְתָּהּ, כְּדֵי שֶׁיֵּעוֹרוּ בָּנָיו וִיהֵא בָהֶם כֹּחַ לְקַבְּלוֹ (ספרי):


כנשר יעיר קנו AS AN EAGLE STIRRETH UP ITS NEST — He guided them with mercy and pity like the eagle which is full of pity towards his young and does not enter its nest suddenly — before it beats and flaps with its wings above its young passing between tree and tree, between branch and branch, in order that its young may awake and have enough strength to receive it (Sifrei Devarim 314:1).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:2)


**יעיר קנו.** יְעוֹרֵר בָּנָיו:


יעיר קנו means, AWAKENS ITS YOUNG (i.e. קן, “nest” is a term for young birds).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:3)


**על גוזליו ירחף.** אֵינוֹ מַכְבִּיד עַצְמוֹ עֲלֵיהֶם אֶלָּא מְחוֹפֵף — נוֹגֵעַ וְאֵינוֹ נוֹגֵעַ (תלמוד ירושלמי חגיגה ב') — אַף הַקָּבָּ"הּ, "שַׁדַּי לֹא מְצָאנֻהוּ שַׂגִּיא כֹחַ" (איוב ל"ז) כְּשֶׁבָּא לִתֵּן תּוֹרָה לֹא נִגְלָה עֲלֵיהֶם מֵרוּחַ אַחַת אֶלָּא מֵאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר "ה' מִסִּינַי בָּא וְזָרַח מִשֵּׂעִיר לָמוֹ הוֹפִיעַ מֵהַר פָּארָן" (דברים ל"ג), "אֱלוֹהַּ מִתֵּימָן יָבוֹא" (חבקוק ג') — זוֹ רוּחַ רְבִיעִית (ספרי):


על גוזליו ירחף IT HOVERETH OVER ITS YOUNG — it does not press heavily upon them, but hovers above them — touching them and yet not touching them. So too, the Holy One, blessed be He, (Job. 37:23) “The Almighty, we did not find Him to be too powerful in strength”: when He came to give the Torah to Israel, He did not reveal Himself from one side, (thus concentrating His power at one point), but from four sides, as it is said, (Deuteronomy 33:2) “The Lord came from Sinai, and shone forth from Seir unto them; He appeared from Mount Paran"; also (Habakkuk 3:3) “God came from Teman” — this is the fourth side (Sifrei Devarim 314:1).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:4)


**יפרש כנפיו יקחהו.** כְּשֶׁבָּא לִטְּלָם מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם אֵינוֹ נוֹטְלָן בְּרַגְלָיו כִּשְׁאָר עוֹפוֹת, לְפִי שֶׁשְּׁאָר עוֹפוֹת יְרֵאִים מִן הַנֶּשֶׁר שֶׁהוּא מַגְבִּיהַּ לָעוּף וּפוֹרֵחַ עֲלֵיהֶם, לְפִיכָךְ נוֹשְׂאָן בְּרַגְלָיו, מִפְּנֵי הַנֶּשֶׁר, אֲבָל הַנֶּשֶׁר אֵינוֹ יָרֵא אֶלָּא מִן הַחֵץ, לְפִיכָךְ נוֹשְׂאָן עַל כְּנָפָיו, אוֹמֵר מוּטָב שֶׁיִּכָּנֵס הַחֵץ בִּי וְלֹא יִכָּנֵס בְּבָנַי, אַף הַקָּבָּ"ה (שמות י"ט) "וָאֶשָּׂא אֶתְכֶם עַל כַּנְפֵי נְשָׁרִים" — כְּשֶׁנָּסְעוּ מִצְרַיִם אַחֲרֵיהֶם וְהִשִּׂיגוּם עַל הַיָּם הָיוּ זוֹרְקִים בָּהֶם חִצִּים וְאַבְנֵי בָּלִיסְטְרָאוֹת, מִיָּד "וַיִּסַּע מַלְאַךְ הָאֱלֹהִים וְגוֹ' וַיָּבֹא בֵּין מַחֲנֵה מִצְרַיִם וְגוֹ'" (שמות י"ד):


יפרש כנפיו יקחהו [AS AN EAGLE …] SPREADETH ABROAD ITS WINGS, TAKETH IT — When it comes to remove them (the young) from one place to another, it does not take them with its claws, as other birds do: because other birds are afraid of the eagle that soars so high and flies above them, therefore they carry them (the young) by their (the mother’s) claws for fear of the eagle. But the eagle is afraid only of an arrow, therefore it carries them (the young) on its wings, saying, “It is better that the arrow pierce me than that it should pierce my young”. So, too, the Holy One, blessed be He, says, (Exodus 19:4) “I bare you as on eagles wings” (Mekhilta): when the Egyptians marched after them and overtook them at the Red Sea, they threw arrows and stone missiles at them, whereupon at once “The angel of God moved … and came between the camp of Egypt [and the camp of Israel]” that it might receive the arrows etc. (Exodus 14:19—20 And Rashi thereon).



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:12:1)


**ה' בדד ינחנו.** נִהֲגָם בַּמִּדְבָּר בָּדָד וּבֶטַח:


ה׳ בדד ינחנו THE LORD ALONE GUIDED THEM — i.e. He guided them in the wilderness alone (unassisted) and yet in security,


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:12:2)


**ואין עמו אל נכר.** לֹא הָיָה כֹחַ בְּאֶחָד מִכָּל אֱלֹהֵי הַגּוֹיִם לְהַרְאוֹת כֹּחוֹ וּלְהִלָּחֵם עִמָּהֶם. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דְרָשׁוּהוּ עַל הֶעָתִיד וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם אֻנְקְלוֹס, וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר דִּבְרֵי תּוֹכֵחָה הֵם לְהָעִיד הַשָּׁמַיִם וְהָאָרֶץ וּתְהֵא הַשִּׁירָה לָהֶם לְעֵד שֶׁסּוֹפָן לִבְגֹּד וְלֹא יִזְכְּרוּ לֹא הָרִאשׁוֹנוֹת שֶׁעָשָׂה לָהֶם וְלֹא הַנּוֹלָדוֹת שֶׁהוּא עָתִיד לַעֲשׂוֹת לָהֶם, לְפִיכָךְ צָרִיךְ לְיַשֵּׁב הַדָּבָר לְכָאן וּלְכָאן. וְכָל הָעִנְיָן מוּסָב עַל **זכר ימות עולם בינו שנות דר ודר**, כֵּן עָשָׂה לָהֶם וְכֵן עָתִיד לַעֲשׂוֹת, כָּל זֶה הָיָה לָהֶם לִזְכֹּר:


ואין עמו אל נכר AND THERE WAS NO STRANGE GOD WITH HIM — for not one of the gods of the other peoples possessed the power to display its might and to war with them. — Our Rabbis, however, explained it as a promise referring to the future (cf. Sifrei Devarim 315), and so, too, does Onkelos render it. But I say that they (the statements of this chapter) are words of reproof which he said with the view of calling heaven and earth as witnesses against them, and also in order that this song (cf. Deuteronomy 31:21) should be witness, because He knew (Deuteronomy 31:29) that they would in future prove faithless and would bear in mind neither the past (lit., the first) deeds that He performed for them nor those that would come to pass (lit., that would be born), which at a future time He would do for them. For this reason it is necessary to make the text fit in with this and with that (the past and the future). Indeed, the whole section is to be connected with (v. 7) “Remember the days of old (i.e., the past), consider the years of generation after generation” (the future) (cf. Rashi on that verse): Thus has He done for them and thus will He in the future do for them — all this they ought to bear in mind.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:1)


**ירכבהו על במתי ארץ.** כָּל הַמִּקְרָא כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ:


ירכבהו על במתי ארץ HE MADE HIM RIDE ON THE HIGH PLACES OF THE EARTH — The entire text (vv. 13 and 14) is to be understood metaphorically as the Targum has it (i.e., as referring to Eretz Israel).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:2)


**ירכבהו וגו'.** עַל שֵׁם שֶׁאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל גָּבוֹהַּ מִכָּל הָאֲרָצוֹת:


ירכבהו וגו׳ HE MADE HIM RIDE [UPON THE HIGH PLACES] — This expression is used because the land of Israel is higher than all other countries (Sifrei Devarim 316:1).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:3)


**ויאכל תנובת שדי.** אֵלּוּ פֵרוֹת אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁקַּלִּים לָנוּב וּלְהִתְבַּשֵּׁל מִכָּל פֵּרוֹת הָאֲרָצוֹת:


ויאכל תנובת שדי THAT HE MIGHT EAT THE PRODUCE OF THE FIELDS — This refers to the fruits of the land of Israel which are quicker to shoot (נוב) and to ripen than all the fruits of other countries (Sifrei Devarim 316:2).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:4)


**וינקהו דבש מסלע.** מַעֲשֶׂה בְּאֶחָד שֶׁאָמַר לִבְנוֹ בְּסִיכְנִי הָבֵא לִי קְצִיעוֹת מִן הֶחָבִית, הָלַךְ וּמָצָא הַדְּבַשׁ צָף עַל פִּיהָ, אָמַר לוֹ זוֹ שֶׁל דְּבַשׁ הִיא אָמַר לוֹ הַשְׁקַע יָדְךָ לְתוֹכָהּ וְאַתָּה מַעֲלֶה קְצִיעוֹת מִתּוֹכָהּ (ספרי):


וינקהו דבש מסלע AND HE MADE HIM SUCK HONEY OUT OF THE ROCK — Indeed it once happened that a man said to his son In Sichnin (a town in Galilee): “Bring me dried figs from the cask”. He went and found the honey flowing over its brim. He said to him, “But this is a vat of honey (not of figs)”. Thereupon he said to him: “Put your hands deep into it and you will bring up figs from it” (Sifrei Devarim 316:3).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:5)


**במתי ארץ.** לְשׁוֹן גֹּבַהּ:


במתי ארץ is an expression denoting height.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:6)


**שדי.** לְשׁוֹן שָׂדֶה:


שדי has the same meaning as שדה, field.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:7)


**מחלמיש צור.** תָּקְפּוֹ וְחָזְקוֹ שֶׁל סֶלַע, כְּשֶׁאֵינוֹ דָּבוּק לַתֵּיבָה שֶׁלְּאַחֲרָיו נָקוּד חַלָּמִישׁ וּכְשֶׁהוּא דָבוּק נָקוּד חַלְמִישׁ:


מחלמיש צור (lit., out of the flint of the rock) — The meaning is: the strength and the hardness of the rock. — When it is not connected with the following word (that is, when it is used in the absolute state) it is vowelled חַלָּמִישׁ (as in Deuteronomy 8:15). whilst when it is connected (is in the construct state) it is punctuated חַלְמִישׁ as here.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:8)


**ושמן מחלמיש צור.** אֵלּוּ זֵיתִים שֶׁל גּוּשׁ חָלָב (ספרי):


ושמן מחלמיש צור AND OIL OUT OF THE FLINTY ROCK — this refers to the olives of Giscala (a place in Galilee) (Sifrei Devarim 316:4).



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:1)


**חמאת בקר וחלב צאן.** זֶה הָיָה בִּימֵי שְׁלֹמֹה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (מלכים א ד') "עֲשָׂרָה בָקָר בְּרִאִים וְעֶשְׂרִים בָּקָר רְעִי וּמֵאָה צֹאן":


חמאת בקר וחלב צאן [AND HE GAVE THEM TO EAT] CLOTTED CREAM OF THE HERD AND MILK OF THE FLOCK — This was in the days of Solomon, as it states (1 Kings 5:2—3): “[And Solomon’s provision for one day was …] ten fat oxen, and twenty oxen (בקר) out of the pasture, and an hundred sheep (צאן)(Sifrei Devarim 317:1).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:2)


**עם חלב כרים.** זֶה הָיָה בִּימֵי עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (עמוס ו') "וְאֹכְלִים כָּרִים מִצֹּאן":


עם חלב כרים WITH FAT OF FATTENED LAMBS — This was in the days of the “Ten Tribes” (the period of the Northern Kingdom). as it is said, (Amos. 6:4) “Who eat lambs (כרים) of the flock” (Sifrei Devarim 317:1).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:3)


**חלב כליות חטה.** זֶה הָיָה בִּימֵי שְׁלֹמֹה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (מלכים א ה') "וַיְהִי לֶחֶם שְׁלֹמֹה וְגוֹ'":


חלב כליות חטה [WITH] THE FAT OF KIDNEYS OF WHEAT — This again was in the days of Solomon, as it is said, (1 Kings 5:2) “And Solomon's provision for one day was [thirty measures of fine flour etc.]” (Sifrei Devarim 317:1).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:4)


**ודם ענב תשתה חמר.** בִּימֵי עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים "הַשֹּׁתִים בְּמִזְרְקֵי יַיִן" (עמוס ו'):


ודם ענב תשתה חמר AND THOU SHALT DRINK THE FOAMING BLOOD OF THE GRAPE — this again was in the days of the “Ten Tribes” as it is said, (Amos 6:6) “That drink wine in bowls” (Sifrei Devarim 317:1).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:5)


**חמאת בקר.** הוּא שֻׁמָּן הַנִּקְלָט מֵעַל גַּבֵּי הֶחָלָב:


חמאת בקר is the cream that is gathered from the surface of the milk.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:6)


**וחלב צאן.** חָלָב שֶׁל צֹאן, וּכְשֶׁהוּא דָבוּק נָקוּד חֲלֵב, כְּמוֹ "בַּחֲלֵב אִמּוֹ" (שמות כ"ג):


וחלב צאן means, and milk of sheep (i.e. the first word is in the construct state); whenever it is connected with the following word it is vowelled חֲלַב, as in (Deuteronomy 14:21) “in the milk of (בַּחֲלֵב) its mother”.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:7)


**כרים.** כְּבָשִׂים:


כרים are SHEEP.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:8)


**ואילים.** כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ:


ואילים has its ordinary meaning: RAMS.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:9)


**בני בשן.** שְׁמֵנִים הָיוּ:


בני בשן RAMS OF THE BREED OF BASHAN — These were very fat.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:10)


**כליות חטה.** חִטִּים שְׁמֵנִים כְּחֵלֶב כְּלָיוֹת וְגַסִּין כְּכוּלְיָא:


כליות חטה KIDNEYS OF WHEAT — wheat that is fat as is the fat of kidneys and the kernels of which are as large as kidneys.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:11)


**ודם ענב.** תִּהְיֶה שׁוֹתֶה יַיִן טוֹב וְטוֹעֵם יַיִן חָשׁוּב:


ודם ענב AND THE BLOOD OF THE GRAPE [THOU SHALT DRINK חמר] — This means: Thou shalt drink good wine, and taste wine of a fine and good quality.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:12)


**חמר.** יַיִן בִּלְשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי "חֲמַר", אֵין זֶה שֵׁם דָּבָר אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן מְשֻׁבָּח בְּטַעַם, ווי"נוש בְּלַעַז. וְעוֹד יֵשׁ לְפָרֵשׁ שְׁנֵי מִקְרָאוֹת הַלָּלוּ אַחַר תַּרְגּוּם שֶׁל אֻנְקְלוֹס "אַשְׁרִינוּן עַל תָּקְפֵּי אַרְעָא וְגוֹ'":


חמר — This word means wine in general in the Aramaic language, but this (the word חמר in our verse) is not a noun but it means excellent in taste, vinos in old French, (English = vinous). — One may also expound these two verses according to the Targum of Onkelos which has: אשרינון על תקפי ארעא וגו׳, He made them dwell upon the strong places of the earth etc.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:1)


**עבית.** לְשׁוֹן עֹבִי:


עבית has the meaning of “thickness” (stoutness), it therefore means: “ Thou hast waxed fat”.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:2)


**כשית.** כְּמוֹ כָּסִיתָ, לְשׁוֹן "כִּי כִסָּה פָנָיו בְּחֶלְבּוֹ" (איוב ט"ו), כְּאָדָם שֶׁשָּׁמֵן מִבִּפְנִים וּכְסָלָיו נִכְפָּלִים מִבַּחוּץ, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר (שם) "וַיַּעַשׂ פִּימָה עֲלֵי כָסֶל":


כשית is the same as כסית (‎ס and ש interchanging). It has the same meaning as in Job 15:27 “Because he hath covered (כסה) his face with his fatness”— like a person who is full of fat inside and whose flanks therefore lie in folds outside, as it goes on to state, (Job 15:27) “and be made folds of fat on his flanks” (Sifrei Devarim 318:6).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:3)


**כשית.** יֵשׁ לָשׁוֹן קַל בִּלְשׁוֹן כִּסּוּי, כְּמוֹ "וְכֹסֶה קָלוֹן עָרוּם" (משלי י"ב), וְאִם כָּתַב כִּשִּׂיתָ, דָּגוּשׁ, הָיָה נִשְׁמָע כִּסִּיתָ אֶת אֲחֵרִים, כְּמוֹ "כִּי כִסָּה פָנָיו" (איוב ט"ו):


כשית — There exists a Kal form (as is this word) in the sense of covering, as (Proverbs 12:16) “And a prudent man is covered with (וְכֹסָה) shame”. If it had written כִּשִּׂיתָ with a dagesh in the ש. it would imply, “Thou hast covered others (or other things)” (i.e., it would be transitive), as in the text already quoted, “For he hath covered (כִסָּה) his face”.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:4)


**וינבל צור ישעתו.** גִּנָּהוּ וּבִזָּהוּ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (יחזקאל ח') "אֲחוֹרֵיהֶם אֶל הֵיכַל ה' וְגוֹ'", אֵין לְךָ נִבּוּל גָּדוֹל מִזֶּה (ספרי):


וינבל צור ישעתו AND HE LIGHTLY ESTEEMED THE ROCK OF HIS SALVATION — This means, He showed contempt for Him and scorned Him, as it is said, (Ezekiel 8:16) “[and, behold, … there were … men] with their buttocks towards the Temple of the Lord” — surely, there can be no greater contempt than this (see Rashi on this verse) (Sifrei Devarim 318:9).



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:16:1)


**יקנאהו.** הִבְעִירוּ חֲמָתוֹ וְקִנְאָתוֹ:


יקנאהו means, they made His anger and His jealousy (קנאה) glow.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:16:2)


**בתועבת.** בְּמַעֲשִׂים תְּעוּבִים, כְּגוֹן מִשְׁכַּב זְכוּר וּכְשָׁפִים שֶׁנֶאֱמַר בָּהֶם תּוֹעֵבָה:


בתועבת WITH ABOMINATIONS — i.e. with abominable deeds such as pederasty and sorcery, of which the term תועבה is used (cf. Leviticus 18:23 and Deuteronomy 18:10—12).



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:17:1)


**לא אלה.** כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ "דְּלֵית בְּהוֹן צְרוֹךְ", אִלּוּ הָיָה בָהֶם צְרוֹךְ לֹא הָיְתָה קִנְאָה כְפוּלָה כְּמוֹ עַכְשָׁו (ספרי):


לא אלה — Understand this as the Targum does: They sacrifice unto devils in which is no utility, for if there were at least any utility in them to the world (as e.g:, the sun, moon and stars) God’s jealousy would not have been so intense (lit. — doubled) as it is now. (Sifrei Devarim 318:13).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:17:2)


**חדשים מקרב באו.** אֲפִלּוּ הָאֻמּוֹת לֹא הָיוּ רְגִילִים בָּהֶם — גּוֹי שֶׁהָיָה רוֹאֶה אוֹתָם, הָיָה אוֹמֵר זֶה צֶלֶם יְהוּדִי:


חדשים מקרב באו [THEY SACRIFICED … TO GODS] THAT CAME UP RECENTLY — i.e. with which even the heathen nations were not familiar. Indeed, if a heathen saw them he would say, “This is a Jewish idol” (Sifrei Devarim 318:14).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:17:3)


**לא שערום אבתיכם.** לֹא יָרְאוּ מֵהֶם — לֹא עָמְדָה שַׂעֲרָתָם מִפְּנֵיהֶם, דֶּרֶךְ שַׂעֲרוֹת הָאָדָם לַעֲמֹד מֵחֲמַת יִרְאָה, כָּךְ נִדְרַשׁ בְּסִפְרֵי. וְיֵשׁ לְפָרֵשׁ עוֹד **שערום** לְשׁוֹן (ישעיה יג, כא) "וּשְׂעִירִים יְרַקְּדוּ שָׁם", שְׂעִירִים הֵם שֵׁדִים, לֹא עָשׂוּ אֲבוֹתֵיכֶם שְׂעִירִים הַלָּלוּ:


לא שערום אבתיכם — This means, which your fathers feared not; more literally it means: Their hair (שער) did not stand up on end because of them — for it is the nature of a person’s hair to stand up out of fear. Thus is it (the word שערום) explained in Sifrei Devarim 318:15. But it is also possible to explain it as being connected with the noun in (Isaiah 13:21) “And שעירים shall dance there”; שעירים are demons (satyrs), and the meaning of our verse would then be: your fathers never made these satyrs.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:18:1)


**תשי.** תִּשְׁכַּח. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דָרְשׁוּ כְּשֶׁבָּא לְהֵיטִיב לָכֶם אַתֶּם מַכְעִיסִין לְפָנָיו וּמַתִּישִׁים כֹּחוֹ מִלְּהֵיטִיב לָכֶם (ספרי):


תשי means THOU HAST FORGOTTEN. But our Rabbis explained it thus: whenever He was about to bestow good upon you, you provoked Him to anger and, as it were, weakened (תש) His power so that He could not do you good (Sifrei Devarim 319:3).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:18:2)


**אל מחללך.** מוֹצִיאֲךָ מֵרֶחֶם, לְשׁוֹן (תהלים כט) "יְחוֹלֵל אַיָּלוֹת", (תהלים מ"ח) "חִיל כַּיּוֹלֵדָה":


אל מחללך means, God Who has brought you forth out of the womb. It has the same meaning as in (Psalms 29:9) “[The voice of the Lord] makes hinds to bring forth (יחולל)”, and (Psalms 48:7) “travail (חיל) as that of a woman in childbirth”.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:1)


**מה אחריתם.** מַה תַּעֲלֶה בָהֶם בְּסוֹפָם:


מה אחריתם [I WILL SEE] WHAT THEIR END SHALL BE — i.e. what will come upon them in the end.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:2)


**כי דור תהפכת המה.** מְהַפְּכִין רְצוֹנִי לְכַעַס:


כי דור תהפכת המה FOR THEY ARE A GENERATION OF PERVERSITY - i.e. they change (הפך) My goodwill into anger.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:3)


**לא אמן בם.** אֵין גִדּוּלַי נִכָּרִים בָּהֶם, כִּי הוֹרֵיתִים דֶּרֶךְ טוֹבָה וְסָרוּ מִמֶּנָּה:


לא אמן בם means, My training is not evident in them, for I showed them the good way and they have deviated from it.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:4)


**אמן.** לְשׁוֹן "וַיְהִי אֹמֵן" (אסתר ב'), נורריטור"ה בְּלַעַז; דָּ"אַ — **אמן** לְשׁוֹן אֱמוּנָה, כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ, אָמְרוּ בְסִינַי נַעֲשֶׂה וְנִשְׁמָע וּלְשָׁעָה קַלָּה בִטְּלוּ הַבְטָחָתָם וְעָשׂוּ הָעֵגֶל (ספרי):


אמן is of the same meaning as in (Esther 2:7) “And he brought up (אמן) Hadassah”. Nouriture in old French — Another explanation of אמן: it has the same meaning as אמונה, faithfulness, as the Targum takes it (“in whom there is no faithfulness”). At Sinai they said, “We will do and we will hearken” and after a very short time they broke their promise and made the golden calf (Sifrei Devarim 320:6).



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:1)


**קנאוני.** הִבְעִירוּ חֲמָתִי:


קנאוני means, they made My anger glow (cf. Rashi on v. 16).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:2)


**בלא אל.** בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ אֱלוֹהַּ:


בלא אל means, with something that is not a god.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:3)


**בלא עם.** בְּאֻמָּה שֶׁאֵין לָהּ שֵׁם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ישעיהו כ"ג) הֵן אֶרֶץ כַּשְׂדִים זֶה הָעָם לֹא הָיָה, בְּעֵשָׂו הוּא אוֹמֵר (עובדיה א') בָּזוּי אַתָּה מְאֹד:


בלא עם [I WILL MOVE THEM TO JEALOUSY] WITH THOSE WHO ARE NOT A PEOPLE — i.e. with a nation that has no reputation, as it states e.g. of the Chaldeans (Isaiah 23:13), “Behold, the land of the Chaldeans, this people was not; [the Assyrians founded it …]”, and of Esau it states (Obadia 1:2) “Thou art greatly despicable”.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:4)


**בגוי נבל אכעיסם.** אֵלּוּ הַמִּינִים, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר (תהילים י"ד) אָמַר נָבָל בְּלִבּוֹ אֵין אֱלֹהִים (ספרי; יבמות ס"ג):


בגוי נבל אכעיסם I WILL PROVOKE THEM TO ANGER WITH A DEBASED NATION — these are the “Minim”, the heretics. So indeed, it states, (Psalms 14:1) “The heretic (נבל) hath said in his heart “There is no God” (Sifrei Devarim 320:10; Yevamot 63b).



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:22:1)


**קדחה.** בָּעֲרָה:


קדחה means, IT BURNS.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:22:2)


**ותיקד.** בָּכֶם עַד הַיְסוֹד:


ותיקד AND IT SHALL BLAZE in your midst to the very foundations (Rashi merely supplies the words “in your midst” and paraphrases עד שאול תחתית).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:22:3)


**ותאכל ארץ ויבלה.** אַרְצְכֶם וִיבוּלָהּ (ספרי):


ותאכל ארץ ויבלה AND IT SHALL CONSUME THE LAND WITH HER INCREASE — i.e., your land and its produce (Rashi means that ארץ does not mean “the ground”, “the soil”).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:22:4)


**ותלהט.** יְרוּשָׁלַיִם הַמְיֻסֶּדֶת עַל הֶהָרִים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (תהילים קכ"ה) יְרוּשָׁלַיִם הָרִים סָבִיב לָהּ:


ותלהט AND IT SHALL SET ON FIRE [THE FOUNDATIONS OF THE MOUNTAINS] — i.e. Jerusalem that is founded on the mountains, as it is said, (Psalms 125:2) “Jerusalem, mountains are round about it” (Sifrei Devarim 320:12).



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:23:1)


**אספה עלימו רעות.** אַחְבִּיר רָעָה עַל רָעָה, לְשׁוֹן "סְפוּ שָׁנָה עַל שָׁנָה" (ישעיהו כ"ט), "סְפוֹת הָרָוָה" (דברים כ"ט), "עֹלוֹתֵיכֶם סְפוּ עַל זִבְחֵיכֶם" (ירמיהו ז'). דָּ"אַ — **אספה** אֲכַלֶּה, כְּמוֹ "פֶּן תִּסָּפֶה" (בראשית י"ט):


אספה עלימו רעות — This means, I will join evil to evil, אספה having the same meaning as in (Isaiah 29:1) “add ye (ספו) year to year”; (Deuteronomy 29:18) “in order to add (ספות) the drunkenness to the thirst”; (Jeremiah 7:21) “Add (ספו) your burnt offerings to your sacrifices”. — Another explanation of אספה: it means, “I will expend” (bring to an end so that there will be no more evils to bring upon them); similar to (Genesis 19:15) “lest thou be brought to an end (תספה).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:23:2)


**חצי אכלה בם.** כָּל חִצַּי אַשְׁלִים בָּהֶם, וּקְלָלָה זוֹ לְפִי הַפֻּרְעָנוּת לִבְרָכָה הִיא — חִצַּי כָּלִים וְהֵם אֵינָם כָּלִים (ספרי; סוטה ט'):


חצי אכלה בם means all my arrows I will use to the full against them (i.e., there will be no arrows left). This curse according to the wording in which the punishment is expressed, implies a blessing: My arrows will come to an end, but they themselves will not come to an end (i.e. they will never be exterminated) (Sifrei Devarim 321:2; Sotah 9a).



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:24:1)


**מזי רעב.** אֻנְקְלוֹס תִּרְגֵּם "נְפִיחֵי כָפָן", וְאֵין לִי עֵד מוֹכִיחַ עָלָיו, וּמִשְּׁמוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי מֹשֶׁה הַדַּרְשָׁן מִטּוּלוּשָׂא שָׁמַעְתִּי שְׂעִירֵי רָעָב — אָדָם כָּחוּשׁ מְגַדֵּל שֵׂעָר עַל בְּשָׂרוֹ:


מזי רעב — Onkelos rendered this by “swollen by famine”. I have, however, no convincing proof for this meaning. In the name of R. Moses the Preacher of Toulouse have heard that it is the same as שעירי רעב, “hairy through hunger”, for an emaciated person grows much hair on his flesh.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:24:2)


**מזי.** לְשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי שֵׂעָר — מִזַיָּא, "דַּהֲוָה מְהַפֵּךְ בְּמִזַיָּא" (מגילה י"ח):


In the Aramaic language the term for hair is מזיא, as e.g. in the phrase (Megillah 18a) “who was busying himself with his hair (במזיא)".


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:24:3)


**ולחמי רשף.** הַשֵּׁדִים נִלְחֲמוּ בָהֶם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר "וּבְנֵי רֶשֶׁף יַגְבִּיהוּ עוּף" (איוב ה'), וְהֵם שֵׁדִים:


ולחמי רשף This means, the demons fight with them (לחם means “to battle”), — as it states, (Job. 5:7) “As the בני רשף fly upwards”, and they are the demons (Berachot 5a).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:24:4)


**וקטב מרירי.** וּכְרִיתוּת שֵׁד שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ מְרִירִי, **קטב** כְּרִיתָה, כְּמוֹ "אֱהִי קָטָבְךָ שְׁאוֹל" (הושע י"ג):


וקטב מרירי means and the destruction caused by a demon whose name is מרירי. The word קטב denotes “cutting off”, “destruction”, as in (Hosea 13:14) “I will be thy destruction (קטבך) O grave”.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:24:5)


**ושן בהמת.** מַעֲשֶׂה הָיָה וְהָיוּ הָרְחֵלִים נוֹשְׁכִין וּמְמִיתִין (ספרי):


ושן בהמת [I WILL SEND FORTH] THE TEETH OF CATTLE [UPON THEM] (i.e., even the teeth of cattle, not only of wild beasts) — It indeed happened once that lambs bit people and caused their death (Sifrei Devarim 321:8; cf. Rashi on Leviticus 26:22).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:24:6)


**חמת זחלי עפר.** אֶרֶס נְחָשִׁים הַמְהַלְּכִים עַל גְּחוֹנָם עַל הֶעָפָר כַּמַּיִם הַזּוֹחֲלִים עַל הָאָרֶץ; זְחִילָה לְשׁוֹן מְרוּצַת הַמַּיִם עַל הֶעָפָר וְכֵן כָּל מְרוּצַת דָּבָר הַמְשַׁפְשֵׁף עַל הֶעָפָר וְהוֹלֵךְ:


חמת זחלי עפר THE FURY OF THOSE CRAWLING IN THE DUST — i.e. the poison of serpents that crawl on their bellies upon the ground as the water trickles (זחל) upon the ground. The root זחל properly denotes the passage of water upon the dust, and it is similarly used as here of the passage of anything that proceeds in a shuffling manner upon the ground.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:25:1)


**מחוץ תשכל חרב.** מִחוּץ לָעִיר תְּשַׁכְּלֵם חֶרֶב גְּיָסוֹת.


מחוץ תשכל חרב WITHOUT, THE SWORD SHALL BEREAVE — This means, outside the city will the sword of the hostile troops bereave them,


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:25:2)


**ומחדרים אימה.** כְּשֶׁבּוֹרֵחַ וְנִמְלָט חַדְרֵי לִבּוֹ נְקוּפִים עָלָיו מֵחֲמַת אֵימָה, וְהוּא מֵת וְהוֹלֵךְ בָּה (ספרי); דָּ"אַ, **ומחדרים אימה** — בַּבַּיִת תִּהְיֶה אֵימַת דֶּבֶר, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ירמיהו ט') "כִּי עָלָה מָוֶת בְּחַלּוֹנֵינוּ", וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם אֻנְקְלוֹס; דָּ"אַ, **מחוץ תשכל חרב** — עַל מַה שֶּׁעָשׂוּ בַחוּצוֹת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר "וּמִסְפַּר חוּצוֹת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם שַׂמְתֶּם מִזְבְּחוֹת לַבֹּשֶׁת" (שם י"א); **ומחדרים אימה**, עַל מַה שֶּׁעָשׂוּ בְחַדְרֵי חֲדָרִים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר "אֲשֶׁר זִקְנֵי בֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל עוֹשִׂים בַּחֹשֶׁךְ אִישׁ בְּחַדְרֵי מַשְׂכִּיתוֹ" (יחזקאל ח'):


ומחדרים אימה AND WITHIN, DREAD — when one flees and escapes, the recesses (חדרים) of his heart will vehemently beat out of dread, and he will gradually die through this (Sifrei Devarim 321:11). — Another explanation of ומחדרים אימה: In the house there will be the fear of pestilence, as it says, (Jeremiah 9:20) “For the death is come up into our windows" (cf. Bava Kamma 60b). So, too, did Onkelos translate it. — Another explanation of this verse: מחוץ תשכל חרב — the sword shall destroy them on account of what they have done in the streets (חוצה; the prefix מ denotes the cause: on account of the streets), as it is said, (Jeremiah 11:13) “According to the number of the streets of Jerusalem have you set up altars to the shameful thing” (Sifrei Devarim 321:12); ומחדרים אימה — on account of what they have done in the innermost chambers of their houses, as it is said, (Ezekiel 8:12) “[Have you seen] what the elders of the house of Israel do in the dark, every man in his marbled chambers?” (Sifrei Devarim 321:12)



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:26:1)


**אמרתי אפאיהם.** אָמַרְתִּי בְלִבִּי אַפְאֶה אוֹתָם, וְיֵשׁ לְפָרֵשׁ **אפאיהם** אֲשִׁיתֵם פֵּאָה, לְהַשְׁלִיכָם מֵעָלַי הֶפְקֵר, וְדֻגְמָתוֹ מָצִינוּ בְעֶזְרָא (נחמיה ט') "וַתִּתֵּן לָהֶם מַמְלָכוֹת וַעֲמָמִים וַתַּחְלְקֵם לְפֵאָה" — לְהֶפְקֵר, וְכֵן חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם. וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִים אוֹתוֹ כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ "יָחוּל רוּגְזִי עֲלֵיהוֹן", וְלֹא יִתָּכֵן, שֶׁאִם כֵּן הָיָה לוֹ לִכְתֹּב אֲאַפְאֵיהֶם, אַחַת לְשִׁמּוּשׁ וְאַחַת לִיסוֹד, כְּמוֹ "אֲאַזֶּרְךָ" (ישעיהו מ"ה), "אֲאַמִּצְכֶם בְּמוֹ פִי" (איוב ט"ו), וְהָאָלֶ"ף הַתִּיכוֹנָה אֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה בוֹ כְלָל. וְאֻנְקְלוֹס תִּרְגֵּם אַחַר לְשׁוֹן הַבָּרַיְתָא הַשְּׁנוּיָה בְסִפְרֵי הַחוֹלֶקֶת תֵּבָה זוֹ לְשָׁלוֹשׁ תֵּבוֹת, אָמַרְתִּי אַף אֵי הֵם — אָמַרְתִּי בְאַפִּי אֶתְּנֵם כְּאִלּוּ אֵינָם שֶׁיֹּאמְרוּ רוֹאֵיהֶם עֲלֵיהֶם אַיֵּה הֵם:


אמרתי אפאיהם means, I said in my heart, “I will אפאה them”. One may explain אפאיהם to mean: I would make them as פאה, i.e. “as grain left in the corner of the field”, to cast them away from Me as something free to all. We find a similar idea to this in Ezra (Nehemiah 9:22), “And thou gavest them kingdoms and nations and didst set them aside as a corner (פאה)” — i.e., as free to all. Thus (under this meaning) did Menachem ben Seruk classify it. Others expound it as the Targum does: My wrath (אף) will fall upon them. This is, however, not correct, for if it were so (i.e., if אפאיהם were connected with אף) it ought to have written אֲאַפְאֵיהֶם with two Alephs, the one as a servile letter (a prefix) and the other as a root letter, just as in (Isaiah 45:5) אאזרך, “I girded thee”, and (Job 16:5) “I would strengthen you (אאמצכם) with my mouth” (in both these cases the first letter of the root is א as it is in אף), and the middle א (that after the פ) would not be proper to be in it at all. Onkelos, however (in connecting the word with אף, anger), rendered it according to the explanation of a Baraitha taught in Sifrei Devarim 322:1 which divides this word into three words, אמרתי אף אי הם, which means, I said in My anger (אף) I will make them as though they were not existent, i.e., that those who are looking for them would ask about them, “Where are they (אי הם)?".



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:27:1)


**לולי כעס אויב אגור.** אִם לֹא שֶׁכַּעַס הָאוֹיֵב כָּנוּס עֲלֵיהֶם לְהַשְׁחִית, וְאִם יוּכַל לָהֶם וְיַשְׁחִיתֵם יִתְלֶה הַגְּדֻלָּה בּוֹ וּבֵאלֹהָיו וְלֹא יִתְלֶה הַגְּדֻלָּה בִּי, וְזֶהוּ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר **פן ינכרו צרימו** — יְנַכְּרוּ הַדָּבָר לִתְלוֹת גְּבוּרָתָם בְּנָכְרִי שֶׁאֵין הַגְּדֻלָּה שֶׁלּוֹ — **פן יאמרו ידנו רמה וגו'**:


לולי כעס אויב אגור WERE IT NOT THAT THE WRATH OF THE ENEMY WAS אגור — i.e. were it not that the wrath of the enemy were heaped up (אגור) against them to destroy them, and if he does overpower them and destroy them, he will attribute the greatness to himself and to his god, and he will not attribute it to Me. That is the meaning of what is stated immediately afterwords: פן ינכרו צרימו — i.e. lest they treat the matter as arising from a stranger (ינכרו) by attributing their power to a stranger (נכרי) to whom the greatness, however, does not actually belong, פן יאמרו ידינו רמה וגו׳ LEST THEY SHOULD SAY, OUR HAND IS EXALTED etc.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:28:1)


**כי** אוֹתוֹ **גוי אבד עצות המה ואין בהם תבונה.** שֶׁאִלּוּ הָיוּ חֲכָמִים **ישכילו זאת איכה ירדף וגו'**:


כי FOR that nation גוי אבד עצות המה ואין בהם תבונה IS A NATION OF ERRING COUNSEL, NEITHER IS THERE ANY UNDERSTANDING IN THEM — for if they were wise (לו חכמו),‎ ישכילו זאת THEY WOULD UNDERSTAND THIS viz., איכה ירדף וגו׳ HOW COULD ONE PURSUE [A THOUSAND] etc.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:29:1)


**יבינו לאחריתם.** יִתְּנוּ לֵב לְהִתְבּוֹנֵן לְסוֹף פֻּרְעֲנוּתָם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


יבינו לאחריתם THEY WOULD CONSIDER THEIR LATTER END — i.e. they would set their heart to comprehend the ultimate reason for the misfortune of the Israelites,



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:30:1)


**איכה ירדף אחד.** מִמֶּנּוּ **אלף** מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל:


איכה ירדף אחד HOW ONE MAN of the nations COULD PURSUE אלף A THOUSAND of the Israelites.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:30:2)


**אם לא כי צורם מכרם וה' הסגירם.** מְכָרָם וּמְסָרָם בְּיָדֵנוּ, דליב"ר בְּלַעַז:


אם לא כי צורם מכרם EXCEPT THAT THEIR ROCK HAD DELIVERED THEM — sold them and delivered them into our hands, 'delivrer' in old French (Eng. = deliver).



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:31:1)


**כי לא כצורנו צורם.** כָּל זֶה הָיָה לָהֶם לָאוֹיְבִים לְהָבִין שֶׁהַשֵּׁם הִסְגִּירָם וְלֹא לָהֶם וְלֵאלֹהֵיהֶם הַנִּצָּחוֹן, שֶׁהֲרֵי עַד הֵנָּה לֹא יָכְלוּ כְּלוּם אֱלֹהֵיהֶם כְּנֶגֶד צוּרֵנוּ, כִּי לֹא כְסַלְעֵנוּ סַלְעָם; כָּל צוּר שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא לְשׁוֹן סֶלַע:


כי לא כצורנו צורם FOR THEIR ROCK IS NOT AS OUR ROCK — all this the enemies should have understood, — that it is the Lord who delivered them (the Israelites) into their hands, and the victory is neither theirs nor their god’s, for until now their gods could achieve nothing against our Rock, “for not as our Rock is their Rock”. — The word צור wherever it occurs in Scripture is the equivalent of סלע, rock.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:31:2)


**ואיבינו פלילים.** וְעַכְשָׁו אוֹיְבֵינוּ שׁוֹפְטִים אוֹתָנוּ, הֲרֵי שֶׁצוּרֵנוּ מְכָרָנוּ לָהֶם:


ואיבינו פלילים AND OUR ENEMIES ARE JUDGES — i.e. are now judges over us; it is evident therefore that our Rock must have delivered us over to them.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:32:1)


**כי מגפן סדם גפנם.** מוּסָב לְמַעְלָה — אָמַרְתִּי בְּלִבִּי אַפְאֵיהֶם וְאַשְׁבִּית זִכְרָם לְפִי שֶׁמַּעֲשֵׂיהֶם מַעֲשֵׂי סְדוֹם וַעֲמוֹרָה:


כי מגפן סדם גפנם FOR THEIR VINE IS OF THE VINE OF SODOM — This is to be connected with what is stated above, (v. 26, verses 27—31 being a parenthesis): “I said in My heart, I would scatter them into corners and make the remembrance of them cease…” because (כי) their doings are as the doings of Sodom and Gomorrah.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:32:2)


**שדמת.** שְׂדֵה תְּבוּאָה, כְּמוֹ "וּשְׁדֵמוֹת לֹא עָשָׂה אֹכֶל" (חבקוק ג'), "בְּשַׁדְמוֹת קִדְרוֹן" (מלכים ב כ"ג):


שדמת denotes fields of grain, as in (Habakkuk 3:17) “and the fields (שדמות) shall yield no corn”; (II Chronicles 23:4) “in the fields (בשדמות) of Kedron”.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:32:3)


**ענבי רוש.** עֵשֶׂב מַר:


ענבי רוש [THEIR GRAPES ARE] GRAPES OF רוש — The word denotes a bitter herb.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:32:4)


**אשכלת מררת למו.** מַשְׁקֶה מַר רָאוּי לָהֶם, לְפִי מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם פֻּרְעֲנוּתָם, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם אֻנְקְלוֹס "וְתוּשְׁלָמַת עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן כִּמְרָרוּתְהוֹן":


אשכלת ממרת למו BITTER CLUSTERS SHALL BE THEIRS — i.e. a bitter drink is what they deserve: according to their actions should be their punishment. Onkelos also renders it thus: “the recompense of their actions shall be according to their bitterness”.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:33:1)


**חמת תנינם יינם.** כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ "הָא כְמָרַת תַּנִּינַיָּא כֹּס פּוּרְעֲנוּתְהוֹן", הִנֵּה כִמְרִירַת נְחָשִׁים כּוֹס מִשְׁתֵּה פֻּרְעֲנוּתָם:


חמת תנינים יינם THEIR WINE IS THE POISON OF DRAGONS — Understand this as the Targum does: הא כמרת תניניא כס פורענותהון which means: Behold, like the gall of serpents is the cup of the drink of their punishment.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:33:2)


**וראש פתנים.** כּוֹסָם שֶׁהוּא **אכזר** לַנָּשׁוּךְ, אוֹיֵב אַכְזָרִי יָבֹא וְיִפָּרַע מֵהֶם:


וראש פתנים AND AS THE VENOM OF VIPERS is their cup — venom, which proves cruel (אכזר) to him who is bitten. The sense is: a cruel enemy will come and exact punishment from them.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:34:1)


**הלא הוא כמס עמדי.** כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ, כִּסְבוּרִים הֵם שֶׁשָּׁכַחְתִּי מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם, כֻּלָּם גְּנוּזִים וּשְׁמוּרִים לְפָנַי:


הלא הוא כמס עמדי IS NOT THIS LAID UP IN STORE WITH ME — Understand this as the Targum has it: (“Are not all their doings open before me?”) — i.e., they believe that I have forgotten their doings, but really they are all stored up and kept with Me.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:34:2)


**הלא הוא.** פְּרִי גַפְנָם וּתְבוּאַת שַׁדְמוֹתָם — **כמס עמדי**:


הלא הוא IS NOT THIS — the fruit of their vine and the produce of their fields (i.e. their doings) כמס עמדי STORED UP WITH ME?



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:1)


**לי נקם ושלם.** עִמִּי נָכוֹן וּמְזֻמָּן פֻּרְעֲנוּת נָקָם, וִישַׁלֵּם לָהֶם כְּמַעֲשֵׂיהֶם, הַנָּקָם יְשַׁלֵּם לָהֶם גְּמוּלָם. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים **ושלם** שֵׁם דָּבָר, כְּמוֹ וְשִׁלּוּם וְהוּא מִגִּזְרַת "וְהַדִּבֵּר אֵין בָּהֶם" (ירמיהו ה') — כְּמוֹ וְהַדִּבּוּר. וְאֵימָתַי אֲשַׁלֵּם לָהֶם?


לי נקם ושלם means, with Me is prepared and held in readiness the punishment of vengeance, and it will recompense them according to their doings. As regards the word ושלם, it means that vengeance will pay them their recompense (וְשִׁלַּם is therefore a verb with the Vau conversive, the subject being the noun נקם which precedes it). — But some explain the word וְשִׁלַּם as a noun, having the same meaning as שִׁלּוּם “recompense”, when it would be of the same grammatical form as the noun in the phrase (Jeremiah 5:13) “And the דִּבֵּר is not in them”, where it has the meaning of דִּבּוּר, “Divine utterance”. And when will I recompense them?


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:2)


**לעת תמוט רגלם.** כְּשֶׁתִּתֹּם זְכוּת אֲבוֹתָם שֶׁהֵן סְמוּכִים עָלָיו:


לעת תמוט רגלם AT THE TIME WHEN THEIR FOOT SHALL TOTTER — i.e. when the merit of their ancestors upon which they are relying (which is, as it were, their foot, their support) will become exhausted.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:3)


**כי קרוב יום אידם.** מִשֶּׁאֶרְצֶה לְהָבִיא עֲלֵיהֶם יוֹם אֵידָם קָרוֹב וּמְזֻמָּן לְפָנַי לְהָבִיא עַל יְדֵי שְׁלוּחִים הַרְבֵּה:


כי קרוב יום אידם FOR THE DAY OF THEIR CALAMITY IS AT HAND — i.e. as soon as I shall desire to bring about the day of their calamity, it will be near at hand and held in readiness by Me — that I may bring it by any of the many agencies I have.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:35:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:4)


**וחש עתדת למו.** וּמַהֵר יָבוֹאוּ הָעֲתִידוֹת לָהֶם:


וחש עתדת למו means, AND THE THINGS THAT ARE TO COME (עתידות) UPON THEM WILL COME QUICKLY (חש).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:35:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:5)


**וחש.** כְּמוֹ "יְמַהֵר יָחִישָׁה" (ישעיהו ה'); עַד כָּאן הֵעִיד עֲלֵיהֶם מֹשֶׁה דִבְרֵי תּוֹכֵחָה לִהְיוֹת הַשִּׁירָה הַזֹּאת לְעֵד, כְּשֶׁתָּבֹא עֲלֵיהֶם הַפֻּרְעָנוּת יֵדְעוּ שֶׁאֲנִי הוֹדַעְתִּים מֵרֹאשׁ, מִכָּאן וָאֵילָךְ הֵעִיד עֲלֵיהֶם דִּבְרֵי תַנְחוּמִין שֶׁיָּבוֹאוּ עֲלֵיהֶם כִּכְלוֹת הַפֻּרְעָנוּת, כְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר אָמַר לְמַעְלָה, "וְהָיָה כִי יָבֹאוּ עָלֶיךָ וְגוֹ' הַבְּרָכָה וְהַקְּלָלָה וְגוֹ' וְשָׁב ה' אֱלֹהֶיךָ אֶת שְׁבוּתְךָ וְגוֹ'":


וחש — The word חש has the same meaning as the verb in (Isaiah V. 19) “Let him make speed and hasten (יחישה)”. — So far Moses exhorted them with words of reproof, so that this song should be witness against them, i.e., that when the punishment would come upon them they would know (acknowledge) that I told them about this from the very beginning. From here onwards he exhorted them with a statement of the comfort that would come upon them when their punishment would have ended; this being similar to all that is stated above (30:1—2) “And it shall come to pass when [all these things] are come upon thee, the blessing and the curse, … then the Lord thy God will turn they captivity, etc.”



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:1)


**כי ידין ה' עמו.** כְּשֶׁיִּשְׁפֹּט אוֹתוֹ בְּיִסּוּרִין הַלָּלוּ הָאֲמוּרוֹת עֲלֵיהֶם, כְּמוֹ "כִּי בָם יָדִין עַמִּים" (איוב ל"ו) — יְיַסֵּר עַמִּים. כִּי זֶה אֵינוֹ מְשַׁמֵּשׁ בִּלְשׁוֹן דְּהָא, לָתֵת טַעַם לַדְּבָרִים שֶׁל מַעְלָה, אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן תְּחִלַּת דִּבּוּר, כְּמוֹ (ויקרא כ"ה) "כִּי תָבֹאוּ אֶל הָאָרֶץ", כְּשֶׁיָּבֹאוּ עֲלֵיהֶם מִשְׁפָּטִים הַלָּלוּ וְיִתְנַחֵם הַקָּבָּ"ה עַל עֲבָדָיו לָשׁוּב וּלְרַחֵם עֲלֵיהֶם:


כי ידין ה' עמו — This means, when He will sentence them to all these sufferings that are mentioned concerning them. The phrase has the same meaning as (Job 36:31) “For by them ידין nations”, i.e. He will chastise the nations. — This word כי has not the meaning of “because”, giving the reason for the preceding statements, but it is an expression (a particle) beginning a new a statement, as (Leviticus 25:2) “When (וכי) ye will come into the land” (cf. Rashi on that passage). The meaning is: When these judgment will come upon them, and the Lord will bethink Himself concerning His servants to have mercy on them again.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:2)


**יתנחם.** לְשׁוֹן הִפּוּךְ הַמַּחֲשָׁבָה לְהֵיטִיב אוֹ לְהָרַע:


יתנחם HE WILL REPENT — This is an expression for changing one’s mind whether for good or for evil.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:3)


**כי יראה כי אזלת יד.** כְּשֶׁיִּרְאֶה כִּי יַד הָאוֹיֵב הוֹלֶכֶת וְחוֹזֶקֶת מְאֹד עֲלֵיהֶם, **ואפס** בָּהֶם **עצור ועזוב**:


כי יראה כי אזלת יד WHEN HE SEETH THAT THEIR POWER IS GOING ON — i.e. when He sees that the power of the enemies is constantly becoming exceedingly stronger over them,ואפס AND THERE IS NOT among them עצור ועזוב.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:4)


**עצור.** נוֹשַׁע עַל יְדֵי עוֹצֵר וּמוֹשֵׁל שֶׁיַּעֲצֹר בָּהֶם:


עצור is one who is saved by a עוצר (a “restrainer”) and ruler who would restrain them,


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:36:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:5)


**עזוב.** עַל יְדֵי עוֹזֵב; עוֹצֵר הוּא הַמּוֹשֵׁל הָעוֹצֵר בָּעָם שֶׁלֹּא יֵלְכוּ מְפֻזָּרִים בְּצֵאתָם לַצָּבָא עַל הָאוֹיֵב, בְּלַעַז מיינטינ"דור; **עצור**, הוּא הַנּוֹשָׁע בְּמַעֲצוֹר הַמּוֹשֵׁל; **עזוב**, מְחֻזָּק, כְּמוֹ (נחום ג') "וַיַּעַזְבוּ יְרוּשָׁלַיִם עַד הַחוֹמָה"; "אֵיךְ לֹא עֻזְּבָה עִיר תְּהִלָּה" (ירמיהו מ"ט), אינפורצדו"ר:


עזוב means one saved by an עוזב. Now an עוצר is a ruler who keeps the people in check that they should not march in scattered groups when they go into war against the enemy, maintenue in old French, (Engl = maintainer). Consequently עצור (the passive) is one who is saved through the restraint of the ruler. עזוב is one who is strengthened, as (Nehemiah 3:8) “and they fortified (ויעזבו) Jerusalem unto the broad wall”; (Jer. 49:25) “How is this city of praise not helped (עזובה)!”. In old French anforcede, (English = fortified).



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:37:1)


**ואמר.** הַקָּבָּ"ה עֲלֵיהֶם **אי אלהימו** שֶׁעָבְדוּ:


ואמר AND HE, the Omnipresent, WILL SAY with regard to them אי אלהימו WHERE ARE THEIR GODS which they served.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:37:2)


**צור חסיו בו.** הַסֶּלַע שֶׁהָיוּ מִתְכַּסִּין בּוֹ מִפְּנֵי הַחַמָּה וְהַצִּנָּה, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁהָיוּ בְטוּחִין בּוֹ לְהָגֵן עֲלֵיהֶם מִן הָרָעָה:


צור חסיו בו THE ROCK IN WHOM THEY TRUSTED — i.e. the rock beneath which they sought shelter (more lit., with which they covered themselves) from the heat and the cold; that is to say, “in which they trusted” that it would protect them against evil.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:38:1)


**אשר חלב זבחימו.** הָיוּ אוֹתָן אֱלֹהוֹת אוֹכְלִים, שֶׁהָיוּ מַקְרִיבִים לִפְנֵיהֶם, וְשׁוֹתִין **יין נסיכם**:


אשר חלב זבחימו THE FAT OF WHOSE SACRIFICES THEY, i.e. these gods — used to eat, which they offered to them, and which used to drink יין נסיכם THE WINE OF THEIR DRINK OFFERING.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:38:2)


**יהי עליכם סתרה.** אוֹתוֹ הַצּוּר יִהְיֶה לָכֶם מַחֲסֶה וּמִסְתּוֹר:


יהי עליכם סתרה LET IT BE YOUR PROTECTION — i.e., let that rock (v. 37) be a refuge and shelter to you.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:1)


**ראו עתה.** הָבִינוּ מִן הַפֻּרְעָנוּת שֶׁהֵבִיא עֲלֵיכֶם וְאֵין לָכֶם מוֹשִׁיעַ, וּמִן הַתְּשׁוּעָה שֶׁאוֹשִׁיעֲכֶם וְאֵין מוֹחֶה בְיָדִי:


ראו עתה SEE NOW — Realise now from the punishment which I have brought upon you and from which there was no one to save you, and, on the other hand, from the help which I will render you and there will be none who can prevent Me,


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:2)


**אני אני הוא.** אֲנִי לְהַשְׁפִּיל וַאֲנִי לְהָרִים:


אני אני הוא [THAT] IT IS I, EVEN I — I am He Who have the power to abase (make low) and it is I who have the power to raise,


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:39:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:3)


**ואין אלהים עמדי.** עוֹמֵד כְּנֶגְדִּי לִמְחוֹת:


ואין אלהים עמדי AND THERE IS NO GOD WITH ME — i.e. standing against Me to prevent this.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:39:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:4)


**עמדי.** דֻּגְמָתִי וְכָמוֹנִי:


עמדי means, who is my equal and like Me.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:39:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:5)


**ואין מידי מציל.** הַפּוֹשְׁעִים בִּי:


ואין מידי מציל AND THERE IS NONE WHO CAN DELIVER OUT OF MY HAND those who rebel against Me.



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:40:1)


**כי אשא אל שמים ידי.** כִּי בַחֲרוֹן אַפִּי אֶשָּׂא יָדִי אֶל עַצְמִי בִּשְׁבוּעָה:


כי אשא אל שמים ידי FOR I SHALL LIFT UP MY HAND TO HEAVEN — This means; for in My wrath I shall lift up My hand to Myself in an oath.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:40:2)


**ואמרתי חי אנכי.** לְשׁוֹן שְׁבוּעָה הוּא, אֲנִי נִשְׁבָּע **חי אנכי**:


ואמרתי חי אנכי — This is the expression of an oath: I SWEAR AS TRUE AS I LIVE [FOR EVER].



### Verse 41

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:41:1)


**אם שנותי ברק חרבי.** אִם אֲשַׁנֵּן אֶת לַהַב חַרְבִּי לְמַעַן הֱיוֹת לָהּ בָּרָק, שפלנ"דור:


אם שנותי ברק חרבי means, IF I SHALL WHET THE BLADE OF MY SWORD, so that it shall have a flash (ברק); splendeur in old French


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:41:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:41:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:41:2)


**ותאחז במשפט ידי.** לְהַנִּיחַ מִדַּת רַחֲמִים מֵאוֹיְבַי שֶׁהֵרֵעוּ לָכֶם "אֲשֶׁר אֲנִי קָצַפְתִּי מְעָט וְהֵמָּה עָזְרוּ לְרָעָה" (זכריה א טו), וְתֹאחֵז יָדִי אֶת מִדַּת הַמִּשְׁפָּט לְהַחֲזִיק בָּהּ וְלִנְקֹם נָקָם:


ותאחז במשפט ידי AND MY HAND TAKE HOLD ON JUDGMENT — i.e., on strict justice, relinquishing (i.e. withdrawing) My attribute of mercy from My enemies who have done so much evil unto you, — “I was angry only a little, but they helped to do harm” (Zechariah 1:15). — but My hand shall grasp the attribute of justice to hold fast to it in order to take vengeance.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:41:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:41:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:41:3)


**אשיב נקם וגו'.** לָמְדוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ בָּאַגָּדָה מִתּוֹךְ לְשׁוֹן הַמִּקְרָא שֶׁאָמַר **ותאחז במשפט ידי** — לֹא כְמִדַּת בָּשָׂר וָדָם מִדַּת הַקָּבָּ"ה, מִדַּת בָּשָׂר וָדָם זוֹרֵק חֵץ וְאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לַהֲשִׁיבוֹ, וְהַקָּבָּ"ה זוֹרֵק חִצָּיו וְיֵשׁ בְּיָדוֹ לַהֲשִׁיבָם כְּאִלּוּ אוֹחֲזָן בְּיָדוֹ, שֶׁהֲרֵי בָרָק הוּא חִצּוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר כָּאן **ברק חרבי ותאחז במשפט ידי**, וְהַמִּשְׁפָּט הַזֶּה לְשׁוֹן פֻּרְעָנוּת הוּא, בְלַעַז יושטיצ"א:


אשיב נקם וגו׳ I WILL RENDER VENGEANCE [TO MY ADVERSARIES]. — Our Rabbis derived in the Agada from the wording of the text which states, “And My hand shall hold the משפט fast”, that the nature of the Holy One, blessed be He, is not like the nature of human beings: The nature of a human being is that when he shoots an arrow he cannot bring it back (i.e., cannot check its flight); the Holy One, blessed be He, however, shoots His arrows and it is in His power to bring them back as though He is still holding them in hand, for, you see, lightning is His arrow, for it states here ברק חרבי, “Lightning is my weapon, and my hand holds fast to משפט" (Mekhilta). This משפט (i.e., משפט according to this explanation) has the meaning of punishment, in old French justicia, the execution of justice (the third definition given by Rashi on Exodus 28:15 of this word)



### Verse 42

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:42:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:42:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:42:1)


**אשכיר חצי מדם.** הָאוֹיֵב:


אשכיר חצי מדם I WILL MAKE MY ARROWS DRUNK WITH BLOOD of the enemy,


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:42:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:42:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:42:2)


**וחרבי תאכל** בְּשָׂרָם:


וחרבי תאכל AND MY SWORD SHALL DEVOUR their FLESH.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:42:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:42:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:42:3)


**מדם חלל ושביה.** זֹאת תִּהְיֶה לָהֶם מֵעֲוֹן דַּם חַלְלֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְשִׁבְיָה שֶׁשָּׁבוּ מֵהֶם:


מדם חלל ושביה FROM THE BLOOD OF THE SLAIN AND THE CAPTIVE i.e., this will happen to them because of the sin of those slain in Israel (i.e., on account of their blood; מדם), and because of the captives they have made amongst them,


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:42:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:42:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:42:4)


**מראש פרעות אויב.** מִפֶּשַׁע תְּחִלַּת פִּרְצוֹת הָאוֹיֵב, כִּי כְּשֶׁהַקָּבָּ"ה נִפְרָע מִן הָאֻמּוֹת פּוֹקֵד עֲלֵיהֶם עֲוֹנָם וַעֲוֹנוֹת אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם מֵרֵאשִׁית פִּרְצָה שֶׁפָּרְצוּ בְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


מראש פרעות אויב — because of the sin of even the very first (ראש) breach (פרעות) made by the enemy (פרצות = פרעות); because when the Holy One, blessed be He, exacts punishment from the heathen nations for the wrongs they have inflicted upon Israel, He visits on them their own sin and the sins of their ancestors, from the very first breach they have made in Israel (Sifrei Devarim 332:5).



### Verse 43

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:1)


**הרנינו גוים עמו.** לְאוֹתוֹ הַזְּמַן יְשַׁבְּחוּ הָאֻמּוֹת אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל, רְאוּ מַה שִּׁבְחָהּ שֶׁל אֻמָּה זוֹ, שֶׁדָּבְקוּ בְּהַקָּבָּ"ה בְּכָל הַתְּלָאוֹת שֶׁעָבְרוּ עֲלֵיהֶם וְלֹא עֲזָבוּהוּ, יוֹדְעִים הָיוּ בְּטוּבוֹ וּבְשִׁבְחוֹ:


הרנינו גוים עמו SING ALOUD, O YE NATIONS, OF HIS PEOPLE — At that time (when I shall take vengeance on them) the nations will praise Israel (Sifrei Devarim 333:2): “See, how praiseworthy is this people, — that they have cleaved to the Holy One, blessed be He, amidst all the troubles which have passed over them, and have not forsaken Him, for they had constantly experienced His goodness and His excellence”.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:2)


**כי דם עבדיו יקום.** שְׁפִיכוּת דְּמֵיהֶם, כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ:


כי דם עבדיו יקם FOR HE WILL AVENGE THE BLOOD OF HIS SERVANTS — i.e., the shedding of their blood, as the phrase usually implies.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:3)


**ונקם ישיב לצריו.** עַל הַגָּזֵל וְעַל הֶחָמָס, כָּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (יואל ד') "מִצְרַיִם לִשְׁמָמָה תִהְיֶה וֶאֱדוֹם לְמִדְבַּר שְׁמָמָה תִּהְיֶה מֵחֲמַס בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה", וְאוֹמֵר (עובדיה י') "מֵחֲמַס אָחִיךָ יַעֲקֹב וְגוֹ'" (עי' ספרי):


ונקם ישיב לצריו AND HE WILL RENDER VENGEANCE TO HIS ADVERSARIES for their robbery and their violence, just as it is stated, (Joel 4:19) “Egypt shall be a desolation and Edom shall be a desolate wilderness, for the violence against the children of Judah …”, and it states in another passage (Obadiah 10) “For the violence against thy brother Jacob [shame shall cover thee]” (cf. Sifrei Devarim 333:3).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:4)


**וכפר אדמתו עמו.** וִיפַיֵּס אַדְמָתוֹ וְעַמּוֹ עַל הַצָּרוֹת שֶׁעָבְרוּ עֲלֵיהֶם וְשֶׁעָשָׂה לָהֶם הָאוֹיֵב:


וכפר אדמתו עמו means, and He will appease His land and His people for the miseries that have passed over them and for that which the enemy has caused them.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:5)


**וכפר.** לְשׁוֹן רִצּוּי וּפִיּוּס, כְּמוֹ (בראשית לב, כא) "אֲכַפְּרָה פָנָיו" — אַנְחִינֵיהּ לְרוּגְזֵיהּ:


וכפר is an expression denoting appeasing and calming, as e.g. (Genesis 32:21) אכפרה פניו which is rendered in the Targum by “I will appease his anger” (cf. Rashi on that verse).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:6)


**וכפר אדמתו** וּמַה הִיא אַדְמָתוֹ? **עמו.** כְּשֶׁעַמּוֹ מִתְנַחֲמִים אַרְצוֹ מִתְנַחֶמֶת, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר "רָצִיתָ ה' אַרְצֶךָ", בַּמֶּה רָצִיתָ אַרְצֶךָ? "שַׁבְתָּ שְׁבִית יַעֲקֹב" (תהילים פ"ה).


וכפר אדמתו AND HE WILL PROPITIATE HIS LAND — and what is His land? עמו HIS PEOPLE — when His people receives comfort His land, too, receives comfort, and so, too, it states, (Psalms 85:2) “Thou hast been favourable, O Lord, unto Thy land” — whereby hast Thou been favourable unto Thy land? The words that follow in the text give the answer: “Thou hast brought back the captivity of Jacob” (Thy people).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:7)


בְּפָנִים אֲחֵרִים הִיא נִדְרֶשֶׁת בְּסִפְרֵי וְנֶחְלְקוּ בָהּ רַבִּי יְהוּדָה וְרַבִּי נְחֶמְיָה, רַבִּי יְהוּדָה דוֹרֵשׁ כֻּלָּהּ כְּנֶגֶד יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְרַבִּי נְחֶמְיָה דוֹרֵשׁ אֶת כֻּלָּה כְּנֶגֶד הָאֻמּוֹת, רַבִּי יְהוּדָה דוֹרְשָׁהּ כְּלַפֵּי יִשְׂרָאֵל — (כו) **אמרתי אפאיהם** כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי, עַד (כז) **ולא ה' פעל כל זאת.** (כח) **כי גוי אבד עצות המה.** אָבְדוּ תוֹרָתִי שֶׁהִיא לָהֶם עֵצָה נְכוֹנָה, **ואין בהם תבונה** לְהִתְבּוֹנֵן (ל) **איכה ירדף אחד** מִן הָאֻמּוֹת **אלף** מֵהֶם, **אם לא כי צורם מכרם.** (לא) **כי לא כצורנו צורם** הַכֹּל כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי עַד תַּכְלִית. וְרַ' נְחֶמְיָה דוֹרְשָׁהּ כְּלַפֵּי הָאֻמּוֹת, **כי גוי אבד עצות המה**, כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי תְחִלָּה עַד **ואיבינו פלילים**:


— In a different manner is it (the section from v. 26 onwards) expounded in Sifrei, and there is a difference of opinion regarding it between R. Judah and R. Nehemiah. R. Judah expounds the whole of it as having reference to Israel, whilst R. Nehemiah refers the whole of it as referring to the other nations (Sifrei Devarim 327). R. Judah explains it as referring to Israel as follows: (26) אמרתי אפאיהם in the same manner as I have explained it, up to (27) כי גוי אבד עצות המה (28) .ולא ה' פעל כל זאת FOR THEY ARE A NATION LOSING COUNSEL, i.e., they have lost My Torah, which was a fitting counsel for them, ואין בהם תבונה AND THERE IS NO UNDERSTANDING IN THEM to consider,# (30) איכה ירדף אחד HOW ONE MAN of the nations COULD PURSUE אלף A THOUSAND of them, אם לא כי צורם מכרם EXCEPT THAT THEIR ROCK HAD DELIVERED THEM into their power.# (31) כי לא כצורנו צורם — all as I have explained, right up to the end. — R. Nehemiah expounds it as referring to the other nations as follows: (28) כי גוי אבד עצות המה — This he explains as I have explained it at first up to (31): ואיבינו פלילים


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:8)


(לב) **כי מגפן סדם גפנם.** שֶׁל אֻמּוֹת:


— (‎32) כי מגפן סדם גפנם FOR AS THE VINE OF SODOM IS THEIR VINE — i.e. the vine of the other nations.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:9)


**ומשדמת עמרה וגו'.** וְלֹא יָשִׂימוּ לִבָּם לִתְלוֹת הַגְּדֻלָּה בִּי:


ומשדמת עמרה AND OF THE FIELDS OF GOMORRAH etc., — and they do not set their mind to attribute the greatness to Me.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:10)


**ענבמו ענבי רוש.** הוּא שֶׁאָמַר **לולי כעס אויב אגור** עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל לְהַרְעִילָם וּלְהַמְרִירָם, לְפִיכָךְ **אשכלת מררת למו** לְהַלְעִיט אוֹתָם עַל מַה שֶּׁעָשׂוּ לְבָנַי:


ענבמו ענבי רוש THEIR GRAPES ARE GRAPES OF GALL — This is what it stated above לולא כעס אויב אגור WERE IT NOT THAT THE ANGER OF THE ENEMY IS HEAPED UP against Israel to destroy them (cf. Rashi’s explanation of אגור above) — so, too, here: "their grapes are grapes of gall”, to make them (the Israelites) reel and to embitter their lives, and therefore אשכלות מררות למו BITTER CLUSTERS ARE FOR THEM to make them swallow them for what they have done to My children.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:11)


(לג) **חמת תנינם יינם.** מוּכָן לְהַשְׁקוֹתָם עַל מַה שֶּׁעוֹשִׂין לָהֶם:


(33) חמת תנינם יינם WINE OF DRAGONS IS THEIR WINE — i.e., the wine prepared to give them to drink for what they have done to them (יינם “their wine” is not the wine which they prepare, but that which is prepared for them).


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:12)


(לד) **כמס עמדי.** אוֹתוֹ הַכּוֹס, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (תהילים ע"ה) "כִּי כוֹס בְּיַד ה' וְגוֹ'":


(34) כמס עמדי IS IT NOT LAID WITH ME — “it” means that cup (which is implied in the word יינם, “the wine prepared for them”), as it states. (Psalms 75:9) “For in the hand of the Lord is a cup … [and the dregs thereof all the wicked of the earth shall drink them]”.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:13)


(לה) **לעת תמוט רגלם.** כָּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר "תִּרְמְסֶנָּה רֶגֶל" (ישעיהו כ"ו):


(35) לעת תמוט רגלם AT THE TIME THEIR FOOT (i.e., of the enemies) SHALL TOTTER — this is similar to what is said, (Isaiah 26:6) “The foot shall tread it down”.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:14)


(לו) **כי ידין ה' עמו.** בְּלָשוֹן זֶה מְשַׁמֵּשׁ **כי ידין** בִּלְשוֹן דְּהָא, וְאֵין יָדִין לְשוֹן יִסּוּרִין אֶלָּא כּמוֹ כִּי יָרִיב אֶת רִיבָם מִיַּד עוֹשְׁקֵיהֶם. **כי יראה כי אזלת יד וגו'**:


(36) כי ידין ה׳ עמו — In this explanation, in the phrase כי ידין the word כי is used in the meaning of “because” (not as in the former explanation where it means “when”), and ידין is not an expression for sufferings (the meaning it was given above) but it means as much as: For He (God) will plead their cause (take their parts) against the power of their oppressors, כי יראה כי אזלת יד וגו׳, when He sees that the hand of the enemy waxes mighty, etc.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:15)


(לז) **ואמר אי אלהימו.** הָאוֹיֵב יֹאמַר אֵי אֱלֹהֵימוֹ שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל? כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר טִיטוּס הָרָשָׁע כְּשֶׁגִּדֵּר אֶת הַפָּרֹכֶת (גיטין נ"ו), כָּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (מיכה ז'): וְתֵרֶא אֹיַבְתִּי וּתְכַסֶּהָ בוּשָׁה הָאוֹמְרָה אֵלַי אַיּוֹ ה' אֱלֹהָיִךְ":


(37) ואמר אי אלהימו AND HE SAYS WHERE IS THEIR GOD — i.e., when the enemy (cf. this with Rashi’s previous explanation) will say, “Where is the God of Israel?” — even as Titus, the wicked, asked, when he pierced the curtain of the Holy of Holies (cf. Gittin 56b), just as it is said, (Micah 7:10) “Then my enemy shall see it, and shame shall cover her who said unto Me, Where is the Lord, thy God?”


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:16)


(לט) **ראו עתה כי אני וגו'.** אָז יְגַלֶּה הַקָּבָּ"ה יְשׁוּעָתוֹ וְיֹאמַר **ראו עתה כי אני אני הוא**, מֵאִתִּי בָּאת עֲלֵיהֶם הָרָעָה וּמֵאִתִּי תָבֹא עֲלֵיהֶם הַטּוֹבָה:


(39) ראו עתה כי אני וגו׳ means, then the Lord will display His saving power and will say, SEE NOW, THAT IT IS I, EVEN I — from Me came the evil upon them (the Israelites) and from Me will good come upon them.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:17)


**ואין מידי מציל.** שֶׁיַּצִּיל אֶתְכֶם מִן הָרָעָה אֲשֶר אָבִיא עֲלֵיכֶם:


ואין מידי מציל AND THERE IS NONE THAT CAN DELIVER OUT OF MY HAND — i.e. that can deliver you (the nations) from the evil which I shall now bring upon you.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:18)


(מ) **כי אשא אל שמים ידי.** כְּמוֹ כִּי נָשָׂאתִי — תָּמִיד אֲנִי מַשְׁרֶה מְקוֹם שְׁכִינָתִי בַשָּׁמַיִם, כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ, אֲפִלּוּ חַלָּש לְמַעְלָה וְגִבּוֹר לְמַטָּה אֵימַת עֶלְיוֹן עַל הַתַּחְתּוֹן וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁגִּבּוֹר לְמַעְלָה וְחַלָּשׁ מִלְּמַטָּה:


(40) כי אשא אל שמים ידי — The first two words (continuing this exposition) are as much as כי נשאתי, “Because I have lifted up”, and the text means: everlasting I made the heaven the dwelling-place of My Glory, — as the Targum takes it (“For I have established in heaven the place of My Divine Glory”) — therefore there will surely be none that can deliver you, for even when the weak person is above and the strong below, the fear of him that is above lies upon him that is below; how much more is this so when “I”, the strong, am above and you, the weak, are below.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:19)


**ידי.** מְקוֹם שְׁכִינָתִי, כְּמוֹ "אִישׁ עַל יָדוֹ" (במדבר ב'), וְהָיָה בְיָדִי לְהִפָּרַע מִכֶּם, אֲבָל אָמַרְתִּי שֶׁחַי אָנֹכִי לְעוֹלָם, אֵינִי מְמַהֵר לִפָּרַע לְפִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לִי שָׁהוּת בַּדָּבָר, אֲנִי חַי לְעוֹלָם וּבְדוֹרוֹת אַחֲרוֹנִים אֲנִי נִפְרָע מֵהֶם וְהַיְכֹלֶת בְּיָדִי לִפָּרַע מִן הַמֵּתִים וּמִן הַחַיִּים; מֶלֶךְ בָּשָׂר וָדָם שֶׁהוּא הוֹלֵךְ לָמוּת מְמַהֵר נִקְמָתוֹ לְהִפָּרַע בְּחַיָּיו כִּי שֶׁמָּא יָמוּת הוּא אוֹ אוֹיְבוֹ וְנִמְצָא שֶׁלֹּא רָאָה נִקְמָתוֹ מִמֶּנּוּ אֲבָל אֲנִי חַי לְעוֹלָם, וְאִם יָמוּתוּ הֵם וְאֵינִי נִפְרָע בְּחַיֵּיהֶם, אֶפָּרַע בְּמוֹתָם:


— ידי means the place of My Divine Glory, as (Numbers 2:17) “every man by his place (ידו)”, — therefore I had the power to punish you immediately, but I said that I shall live forever, therefore I need not hasten to exact punishment, because I have time for this matter: I live for ever and in later generations can punish them, for I have the power to exact punishment both from the living and the dead. A human king who is always going to die (may die at any moment), takes quick vengeance to punish during his lifetime, because either he or his enemy may die with the result that he would never exact his vengeance from him; but I live for ever, and if they (My enemies) should die and I shall not have exacted punishment from them during their lifetime, I can exact it when they are dead.


###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:43:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:20)


(מא) **אם שנותי ברק חרבי.** הַרְבֵּה אִם יֵשׁ שֶׁאֵינָם תְּלוּיִן, כְּשֶׁאֲשַׁנֵּן **ברק חרבי ותאחז במשפט ידי**, כֻּלּוֹ כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי לְמַעְלָה:


(41) אם שנותי ברק חרבי — There are many instances where the particle אם is not used conditionally (i.e., it does not signify “if”). Here, also: when I shall whet ברק חרבי MY GLITTERING SWORD AND MY HAND SHALL HOLD FAST ON JUSTICE, etc., etc., all as I have explained above.



### Verse 44

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:44:1)


**הוא והושע בן נון.** שַׁבָּת שֶׁל דְּיוֹזְגִי הָיְתָה, נִטְּלָה רְשׁוּת מִזֶּה וְנִתְּנָה לָזֶה, הֶעֱמִיד לוֹ מֹשֶׁה מְתֻרְגְּמָן לִיהוֹשֻׁעַ שֶׁיְּהֵא דוֹרֵשׁ בְּחַיָּיו, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יֹאמְרוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּחַיֵּי רַבְּךָ לֹא הָיָה לְךָ לְהָרִים רֹאשׁ; וְלָמָּה קוֹרְאֵהוּ כָּאן הוֹשֵׁעַ? לוֹמַר שֶׁלֹּא זָחָה דַעְתּוֹ עָלָיו שֶׁאַעַ"פִּ שֶׁנִּתְּנָה לוֹ גְּדֻלָּה הִשְׁפִּיל עַצְמוֹ כַּאֲשֶׁר מִתְּחִלָּתוֹ (עי' ספרי):


הוא והושע בן נון HE AND HOSHEA THE SON OF NUN — It was the Sabbath of transmission of office (lit., “of two pairs” of judges): authority was taken from one and given to the other (Sotah 13b). Moses appointed a Meturgeman for Joshua that he (Joshua) should hold in public an Halachic discourse during his (Moses') lifetime in order that the Israelites should not say to him, “During your teacher’s lifetime you did not dare to raise your head!” — And why does it (Scripture) here call him Hosea (since his name had long since been changed to that of Joshua — cf. Numbers 13:16)? In order to indicate that he did not become haughty, for although this dignity had been conferred upon him, he assumed a humble bearing as at the commencement of his career (cf. Sifrei Devarim 334:3).



### Verse 46

###### Rashi on Deuteronomy 32:46:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:46:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:46:1)


**שימו לבבכם.** צָרִיךְ אָדָם שֶׁיִּהְיוּ עֵינָיו וְלִבּוֹ וְאָזְנָיו מְכֻוָּנִים לְדִבְרֵי תוֹרָה, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר "בֶּן אָדָם רְאֵה בְעֵינֶיךָ וּבְאָזְנֶיךָ שְׁמַע וְשִׂים לִבְּךָ" (יחזקאל מ'); וַהֲרֵי דְבָרִים קַל וָחֹמֶר וּמַה תַּבְנִית הַבַּיִת שֶׁהוּא נִרְאֶה לָעֵינַיִם וְנִמְדָד בְּקָנֶה צָרִיךְ אָדָם שֶׁיִּהְיוּ עֵינָיו וְאָזְנָיו וְלִבּוֹ מְכֻוָּנִין, לְהָבִין דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה שֶׁהֵן כַּהֲרָרִין תְּלוּיִין בִּשְׂעָרָה עַל אַחַת כַּמָּה וְכַמָּה (ספרי):
